It just pulls me away. I'm off doing something else before I even know it. So... no.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like your thoughts are not making sense to you or are hard to follow?\n\nPatient: I... I don't really know. They're just my thoughts. I guess they make sense to me, even if there's a lot of them. They're just mine. Nobody is... making me think them. I can't really slow it down.\n\nDoctor: Do you find it hard to keep your thoughts in order when you're talking to someone?\n\nPatient: I don't think so. I just... say what I need to.
